--------------------------------- Interface Id IPV4.State IPV6.State MTU Type DIS Vlanif10 001 Up Down 1497 L1/L2 No/No \# 查看DeviceD的IS-IS接口信息。
[DeviceD] display isis interface Interface information for ISIS(1)
--------------------------------- Interface Id IPV4.State IPV6.State MTU Type DIS Vlanif10 001 Up Down 1497 L2 No/Yes说明从接口信息中可以看到，在使用缺省DIS优先级的情况下，在Level-1级别的设备中，设备B上接口的MAC地址最大，因此设备B为Level-1的DIS；在Level-2级别的设备中，设备D上接口的MAC地址最大，因此设备D为Level-2的DIS。Level-1和Level-2的伪节点分别是0000.0000.0002.01和0000.0000.0004.01。
步骤4 配置设备A的DIS优先级。
[DeviceA] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] isis dis-priority 100 \# 查看设备A的IS-IS邻居信息。
[DeviceA] display isis peer Peer information for ISIS(1)
---------------------------- System Id Interface Circuit Id State HoldTime Type PRI
----------------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000.0000.0002 Vlanif10 0000.0000.0001.01 Up 21s L1(L1L2) 64 0000.0000.0003 Vlanif10 0000.0000.0001.01 Up 27s L1 64 0000.0000.0002 Vlanif10 0000.0000.0001.01 Up 28s L2(L1L2) 64 0000.0000.0004 Vlanif10 0000.0000.0001.01 Up 30s L2 64 Total Peer(s): 4
----结束

检查配置结果\#查看DeviceA设备上的IS-IS接口信息。
[DeviceA] display isis interface Interface information for ISIS(1)
--------------------------------- Interface Id IPV4.State IPV6.State MTU Type DIS Vlanif10 001 Up Down 1497 L1/L2 Yes/Yes说明从上述信息中可以看到，在改变IS-IS接口的DIS优先级后，DeviceA立即成为Level-1-2的DIS，且伪节点是0000.0000.0001.01。
\# 查看DeviceB设备上的IS-IS邻居和接口信息。
[DeviceB] display isis peer Peer information for ISIS(1)
---------------------------- System Id Interface Circuit Id State HoldTime Type PRI
----------------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000.0000.0001 Vlanif10 0000.0000.0001.01 Up 7s L1(L1L2) 100 0000.0000.0003 Vlanif10 0000.0000.0001.01 Up 25s L1 64 0000.0000.0001 Vlanif10 0000.0000.0001.01 Up 7s L2(L1L2) 100 0000.0000.0004 Vlanif10 0000.0000.0001.01 Up 25s L2 64 Total Peer(s): 4 [RouterB] display isis interface Interface information for ISIS(1)
--------------------------------- Interface Id IPV4.State IPV6.State MTU Type DIS Vlanif10 001 Up Down 1497 L1/L2 No/No \# 查看DeviceD设备上的IS-IS邻居和接口信息。
[DeviceD] display isis peer Peer information for ISIS(1)
---------------------------- System Id Interface Circuit Id State HoldTime Type PRI
----------------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000.0000.0001 Vlanif10 0000.0000.0001.01 Up 9s L2 100 0000.0000.0002 Vlanif10 0000.0000.0001.01 Up 28s L2 64 Total Peer(s): 2 [RouterD] display isis interface Interface information for ISIS(1)
--------------------------------- Interface Id IPV4.State IPV6.State MTU Type DIS Vlanif10 001 Up Down 1497 L1/L2 No/No配置脚本
● DeviceA \# sysname DeviceA \# vlan batch 10 \# isis 1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 10.1.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 isis dis-priority 100

\# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# return
● DeviceB \# sysname DeviceB \# vlan batch 10 \# isis 1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0002.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 10.1.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# return
● DeviceC \# sysname DeviceC \# vlan batch 10 \# isis 1 is-level level-1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0003.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 10.1.1.3 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# return
● DeviceD \# sysname DeviceD \# vlan batch 10 \# isis 1 is-level level-2 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0004.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 10.1.1.4 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# return

#### 9.39.4 举例：配置IS-IS的路由渗透功能

组网需求如图9-52所示，网络中的六台设备都配置了IS-IS路由协议。其中DeviceA、DeviceB作为Level-1设备，DeviceC、DeviceD作为Level-1–2设备都属于Area10。DeviceE、DeviceF作为Level-2设备属于Area20。除了DeviceC上GE1/0/0接口的开销值被设为40，其他所有接口的开销值都为缺省值10。要求将Level-2区域中的路由信息渗透到Level-1区域，使得DeviceA到DeviceF的路由为最优路由。
图 配置 的路由渗透功能组网图9-52 IS-IS说明本例中interface1，interface2和interface3分别代表Vlanif10，Vlanif20，Vlanif30。
配置注意事项为了提升安全性，推荐部署IS-IS认证功能（参见“配置IS-IS认证”）。其中，以配置IS-IS 接口认证为例，详细配置方法请参见“举例：配置 IS-IS 基本功能”。
配置思路采用如下的思路配置IS-IS路由参透：
● 配置各设备的接口IP地址以及IS-IS路由协议，实现网络互联。
● 配置DeviceA、DeviceB作为Level-1设备，DeviceC、DeviceD作为Level-1–2设备都属于Area10。配置DeviceE、DeviceF作为Level-2设备属于Area20。
● 配置DeviceC上GE1/0/0接口的开销值为40，其他所有接口的开销值都为缺省值10。
● 配置将Level-2区域中的路由信息渗透到Level-1区域，使得DeviceA到DeviceF的路由为最优路由。

操作步骤步骤1 配置各路由设备接口IPv4地址。
\# 配置DeviceA。
<HUAWEI> system-view [HUAWEI] sysname DeviceA [DeviceA] vlan batch 10 20 [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/1 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] quit [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/2 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] ip address 192.168.1.1 24 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 20 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] ip address 192.168.2.1 24 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] quit DeviceB 、 DeviceC 、 DeviceD 、 DeviceE 和 DeviceF 的配置过程与 DeviceA 类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤2 在各设备上配置IS-IS基本功能。
\# 配置DeviceA。
[DeviceA] isis 10 [DeviceA-isis-10] is-level level-1 [DeviceA-isis-10] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 [DeviceA-isis-10] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] isis enable 10 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] quit \# 配置DeviceB。
[DeviceB] isis 10 [DeviceB-isis-10] is-level level-1 [DeviceB-isis-10] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0002.00 [DeviceB-isis-10] quit [DeviceB] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceB-Vlanif10] isis enable 10 [DeviceB-Vlanif10] quit \# 配置DeviceC。
[DeviceC] isis 10 [DeviceC-isis-10] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0003.00 [DeviceC-isis-10] quit [DeviceC] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceC-Vlanif10] isis enable 10 [DeviceC-Vlanif10] isis cost 40 level-2 [DeviceC-Vlanif10] quit \# 配置DeviceD。
[DeviceD] isis 10 [DeviceD-isis-10] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0004.00 [DeviceD-isis-10] quit [DeviceD] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceD-Vlanif10] isis enable 10 [DeviceD-Vlanif10] quit \# 配置DeviceE。

[DeviceE] isis 10 [DeviceE-isis-10] is-level level-2 [DeviceE-isis-10] network-entity 20.0000.0000.0005.00 [DeviceE-isis-10] quit [DeviceE] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceE-Vlanif10] isis enable 10 [DeviceE-Vlanif10] quit \# 配置DeviceF。
[DeviceF] isis 10 [DeviceF-isis-10] is-level level-2 [DeviceF-isis-10] network-entity 20.0000.0000.0006.00 [DeviceF-isis-10] quit [DeviceF] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceF-Vlanif10] isis enable 10 [DeviceF-Vlanif10] quit Vlanif20和Vlanif30的配置与Vlanif10相同，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤3 在DeviceD上配置路由渗透。
[DeviceD] isis 10 [DeviceD-isis-10] import-route isis level-2 into level-1 [DeviceD-isis-10] quit
----结束检查配置结果\# 在DeviceD上配置路由渗透之前，在DeviceA上执行命令tracert 192.168.6.2，发现从DeviceA到DeviceF的路径为DeviceA->DeviceC->DeviceE->DeviceF。整条链路的开销值为10+40+10=60。
\# 在DeviceD上配置路由渗透之后，在DeviceA上执行命令tracert 192.168.6.2，发现从DeviceA到DeviceF的路径为DeviceA->DeviceB->DeviceD->DeviceE->DeviceF。此时整条链路的开销值为10+10+10+10=40。由此可知配置路由渗透后，DeviceA到DeviceF在路由为最优路由。
配置脚本
● DeviceA \# sysname DeviceA \# vlan batch 10 20 \# isis 10 is-level level-1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 192.168.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 \# interface Vlanif20 ip address 192.168.2.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 20

\# return
● DeviceB \# sysname DeviceB \# vlan batch 10 20 \# isis 10 is-level level-1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0002.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 192.168.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 \# interface Vlanif20 ip address 192.168.3.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 \# return
● DeviceC \# sysname DeviceC \# vlan batch 10 20 \# isis 10 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0003.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 192.168.4.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 isis cost 40 level-2 \# interface Vlanif20 ip address 192.168.2.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 \# return
● DeviceD \# sysname DeviceD \# vlan batch 10 20 \# isis 10 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0004.00 import-route isis level-2 into level-1 \#

interface Vlanif10 ip address 192.168.3.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 \# interface Vlanif20 ip address 192.168.5.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 \# return
● DeviceE \# sysname DeviceE \# vlan batch 10 20 30 \# isis 10 is-level level-2 network-entity 20.0000.0000.0005.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 192.168.4.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 \# interface Vlanif20 ip address 192.168.5.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 \# interface Vlanif30 ip address 192.168.6.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 \# interface 10GE1/0/3 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 30 \# return
● DeviceF \# sysname DeviceF \# vlan batch 10 \# isis 10 is-level level-2 network-entity 20.0000.0000.0006.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 192.168.6.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk

port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# return

#### 9.39.5 举例：配置IS-IS负载分担

组网需求如图9-53所示，DeviceA、DeviceB、DeviceC和DeviceD之间通过IS-IS协议达到IP网络互连的目的，DeviceA、DeviceB、DeviceC和DeviceD同属于区域10，都是Level-2设备，配置负载分担，使得DeviceA的流量，可以分别通过设备DeviceB和DeviceC发送到设备DeviceD。
图 9-53 配置 IS-IS 负载分担组网图说明本例中interface1，interface2和interface3分别代表Vlanif100，Vlanif200，Vlanif300。
配置注意事项为了提升安全性，推荐部署IS-IS认证功能（参见“配置IS-IS认证”）。其中，以配置IS-IS接口认证为例，详细配置方法请参见“举例：配置IS-IS基本功能”。
配置思路采用如下的思路配置IS-IS的负载分担：
1. 配置各路由设备接口IPv4地址。
2. 在各设备上使能 IS-IS 基本功能，实现互连。
3. 在DeviceA上取消负载分担，查看路由表信息。
4. 在DeviceA上恢复负载分担路由的缺省数目，查看路由表信息。

操作步骤步骤1 配置各路由设备接口IPv4地址。
\# 配置DeviceA。
<HUAWEI> system-view [HUAWEI] sysname DeviceA [DeviceA] vlan batch 100 200 300 [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/1 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] quit [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/2 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] quit [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/3 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/3] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/3] port trunk allow-pass vlan 300 [DeviceA] interface Vlanif 100 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] ip address 10.1.1.1 255.255.255.0 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceA] interface Vlanif 200 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] ip address 10.1.2.1 255.255.255.0 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] quit [DeviceA] interface Vlanif 300 [DeviceA-Vlanif300] ip address 172.16.1.1 255.255.255.0 [DeviceA-Vlanif300] quit DeviceB，DeviceC和DeviceD的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤2 配置IS-IS基本功能。
\# 配置DeviceA。
[DeviceA] isis 1 [DeviceA-isis-1] is-level level-1 [DeviceA-isis-1] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 [DeviceA-isis-1] quit [DeviceA] interface Vlanif 100 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] isis enable 1 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceA] interface Vlanif 200 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] isis enable 1 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] quit [DeviceA] interface Vlanif 300 [DeviceA-Vlanif300] isis enable 1 [DeviceA-Vlanif300] quit DeviceB，DeviceC和DeviceD的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤3 在DeviceA上取消负载分担，即配置负载分担的等价路由最大数目为1。
[DeviceA] isis 1 [DeviceA-isis-1] maximum load-balancing 1 [DeviceA-isis-1] quit \# 查看DeviceA的路由表。
[DeviceA] display isis route Route information for ISIS(1)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------- Flags: D-Direct, A-Added to URT, L-Advertised in LSPs, S-IGP Shortcut, U-Up/Down Bit Set Protect Type: L-Link Protect, N-Node Protect

ISIS(1) Level-2 Forwarding Table
---------------------------------------------------------------------------- IPV4 Destination IntCost ExtCost ExitInterface NextHop Flags
---------------------------------------------------------------------------
192.168.1.0/24 20 NULL Vlanif200 10.1.2.2 A/-/-/-/-
10.1.1.0/24 10 NULL Vlanif100 Direct D/-/L/-/-
172.16.1.0/24 10 NULL Vlanif300 Direct D/-/L/-/-
172.16.2.0/24 30 NULL Vlanif100 10.1.1.2 A/-/-/-/-
10.1.2.0/24 10 NULL Vlanif200 Direct D/-/L/-/-
192.168.0.0/24 20 NULL Vlanif100 10.1.1.2 A/-/-/-/-从路由表中可以看出，当配置负载分担最大等价路由条数为1后，到达目标网段
172.16.2.0的下一跳为10.1.1.2，这是由于DeviceB的System ID较小，所以IS-IS优先选择下一跳为10.1.1.2为唯一最优路由。
步骤4 在DeviceA上恢复负载分担路由的缺省数目。
[DeviceA] isis 1 [DeviceA-isis-1] undo maximum load-balancing [DeviceA-isis-1] quit
----结束检查配置结果\# 在DeviceA上恢复负载分担路由的缺省数目，查看DeviceA的路由表。
[DeviceA] display isis route Route information for ISIS(1)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------- Flags: D-Direct, A-Added to URT, L-Advertised in LSPs, S-IGP Shortcut, U-Up/Down Bit Set Protect Type: L-Link Protect, N-Node Protect ISIS(1) Level-2 Forwarding Table
---------------------------------------------------------------------------- IPV4 Destination IntCost ExtCost ExitInterface NextHop Flags
---------------------------------------------------------------------------
192.168.1.0/24 20 NULL Vlanif200 10.1.2.2 A/-/-/-/-
10.1.1.0/24 10 NULL Vlanif100 Direct D/-/L/-/-
172.16.1.0/24 10 NULL Vlanif300 Direct D/-/L/-/-
172.16.2.0/24 30 NULL Vlanif100 10.1.1.2 A/-/-/-/- Vlanif200 10.1.2.2
10.1.2.0/24 10 NULL Vlanif200 Direct D/-/L/-/-
192.168.0.0/24 20 NULL Vlanif100 10.1.1.2 A/-/-/-/-从路由表可以看出，当取消负载分担设置即恢复缺省配置后，由于最大等价路由条数为32，因此设备DeviceA的两个下一跳10.1.1.2（DeviceB）和10.1.2.2（DeviceC）均成为有效路由。
配置脚本
● DeviceA \# sysname DeviceA \# vlan batch 100 200 300 \# isis 1 is-level level-2 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 10.1.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1

\# interface Vlanif200 ip address 10.1.2.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif300 ip address 172.16.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# interface 10GE1/0/3 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 300 \# return
● DeviceB \# sysname DeviceB \# vlan batch 100 200 \# isis 1 is-level level-2 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0002.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 10.1.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif200 ip address 192.168.0.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# return
● DeviceC \# sysname DeviceC \# vlan batch 100 200 \# isis 1 is-level level-2 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0003.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 10.1.2.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 100 \# interface Vlanif200 ip address 192.168.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/1

port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# return
● DeviceD \# sysname DeviceD \# vlan batch 100 200 300 \# isis 1 is-level level-2 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0004.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 192.168.0.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif200 ip address 192.168.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif300 ip address 172.16.2.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# interface 10GE1/0/3 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 300 \# return

#### 9.39.6 举例：配置IS-IS引入BGP路由

组网需求如图 9-54 所示， DeviceA 和 DeviceB 属于同一自治系统，两者之间建立 IS-IS 邻居。
DeviceA为AS内部的一台非BGP设备，DeviceB和DeviceC之间建立EBGP连接。要求IS- IS协议引入BGP路由时，通过应用路由策略改变路由的Cost。
图 9-54 配置 IS-IS 引入 BGP 路由组网图说明本例中interface1，interface2分别代表Vlanif100，Vlanif200。

配置注意事项为了提升安全性，推荐部署 IS-IS 认证功能（参见“配置 IS-IS 认证”）。其中，以配置IS-IS接口认证为例，详细配置方法请参见“举例：配置IS-IS基本功能”。
配置思路采用如下的思路配置IS-IS和BGP交互：
1. 配置各路由设备接口IPv4地址。
2. 在DeviceA和DeviceB上使能IS-IS，指定网络实体。
3. 在DeviceB和DeviceC上配置EBGP连接。
4. 在DeviceB上配置BGP与IS-IS互相引入，查看路由信息。
操作步骤步骤1 配置各路由设备接口IPv4地址。
\# 配置DeviceA。
<HUAWEI> system-view [HUAWEI] sysname DeviceA [DeviceA] vlan batch 100 [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/1 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] quit [DeviceA] interface Vlanif 100 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] ip address 10.1.1.1 255.255.255.0 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] quit DeviceB，DeviceC和DeviceD的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤2 在DeviceA和DeviceB上使能IS-IS，指定网络实体。
\# 配置 DeviceA 。
[DeviceA] isis 1 [DeviceA-isis-1] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 [DeviceA-isis-1] quit

[DeviceA] interface Vlanif 100 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] isis enable 1 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] quit DeviceB的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤3 在DeviceB和DeviceC上配置EBGP连接。
\# 配置DeviceB。
[DeviceB] bgp 65008 [DeviceB-bgp] router-id 1.1.1.1 [DeviceB-bgp] peer 10.2.1.2 as-number 65009 [DeviceB-bgp] ipv4-family unicast [DeviceB-bgp-af-ipv4] network 10.2.1.0 255.255.255.0 [DeviceB-bgp-af-ipv4] quit \# 配置DeviceC。
[DeviceC] bgp 65009 [DeviceC-bgp] router-id 2.2.2.2 [DeviceC-bgp] peer 10.2.1.1 as-number 65008 [DeviceC-bgp] ipv4-family unicast [DeviceC-bgp-af-ipv4] network 10.2.1.0 255.255.255.0 [DeviceC-bgp-af-ipv4] quit步骤4 配置IS-IS引入BGP路由。
\# 在DeviceC上配置一条静态路由。
[DeviceC] ip route-static 172.16.1.1 32 NULL 0 \# 在DeviceC上配置BGP引入静态路由。
[DeviceC] bgp 65009 [DeviceC-bgp] import-route static [DeviceC-bgp] quit \# 在DeviceB上配置IS-IS引入BGP路由。
[DeviceB] isis 1 [DeviceB-isis-1] import-route bgp [DeviceB-isis-1] quit \# 查看DeviceA的路由表，IS-IS引入BGP路由172.16.1.1/32成功。
[DeviceA] display ip routing-table Route Flags: R - relay, D - download to fib, T - to vpn-instance, B - black hole route
------------------------------------------------------------------------------ Routing Table: _public_ Destinations : 6 Routes : 6 Destination/Mask Proto Pre Cost Flags NextHop Interface
10.1.1.0/24 Direct 0 0 D 10.1.1.1 Vlanif100
10.1.1.1/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 Vlanif100
10.1.1.255/32 Direct 0 0 D 10.1.1.2 Vlanif100
127.0.0.0/8 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0
127.0.0.1/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0
172.16.1.1/32 ISIS 15 74 D 10.1.1.2 Vlanif100
127.255.255.255/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0
255.255.255.255/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0 \# 在DeviceB上配置AS_Path属性过滤器并在路由策略RTC中应用。
[DeviceB] ip as-path-filter 1 permit 65009 [DeviceB] route-policy RTC permit node 0 [DeviceB-route-policy] if-match as-path-filter 1 [DeviceB-route-policy] apply cost + 20 [DeviceB-route-policy] quit

\# 在DeviceB上配置IS-IS引入BGP路由。
[DeviceB] isis 1 [DeviceB-isis-1] import-route bgp route-policy RTC [DeviceB-isis-1] quit \# 查看DeviceA的路由表，AS_Path属性过滤应用成功，引入路由172.16.1.1/32的Cost值从74变为94。
[DeviceA] display ip routing-table Route Flags: R - relay, D - download to fib, T - to vpn-instance, B - black hole route

[DeviceC] display ip routing-table Route Flags: R - relay, D - download to fib, T - to vpn-instance, B - black hole route

配置脚本
● DeviceA \# sysname DeviceA \# vlan batch 100

\# isis 1 is-level level-1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 10.1.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# return
● DeviceB \# sysname DeviceB \# vlan batch 100 200 \# isis 1 import-route bgp route-policy RTC network-entity 10.0000.0000.0002.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 10.1.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif200 ip address 10.2.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# interface LoopBack0 ip address 1.1.1.1 255.255.255.255 \# bgp 65008 router-id 1.1.1.1 peer 10.2.1.2 as-number 65009 \# ipv4-family unicast undo synchronization network 10.2.1.0 255.255.255.0 import-route static import-route isis 1 peer 10.2.1.2 enable \# ip as-path-filter 1 index 10 permit 65009 \# route-policy RTC permit node 0 if-match as-path-filter 1 apply cost + 20 \# return
● DeviceC \# sysname DeviceC \# vlan batch 100 \# interface Vlanif100

ip address 10.2.1.2 255.255.255.0 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface LoopBack0 ip address 2.2.2.2 255.255.255.255 \# bgp 65009 router-id 2.2.2.2 peer 10.2.1.1 as-number 65008 \# ipv4-family unicast undo synchronization network 10.2.1.0 255.255.255.0 import-route static peer 10.2.1.1 enable \# ip route-static 172.16.1.1 255.255.255.255 NULL0 \# return

#### 9.39.7 举例：配置IS-IS的路由聚合

组网需求如图9-55所示，设备DeviceA、DeviceB和DeviceC都运行IS-IS路由协议实现互连。
DeviceB将Level-1区域的3个网段（172.16.1.0/24、172.16.2.0/24和172.16.3.0/24）的路由渗透到Level-2区域。在DeviceB上配置对以上3个Level-1区域网段的路由进行路由聚合，即将以上3条路由聚合为一条路由172.16.0.0/16，可以减少DeviceB的路由表条目，也可以降低Level-1区域的链路抖动对Level-2区域的路由收敛的影响。
图 9-55 配置 IS-IS 的路由聚合组网图说明本例中interface1，interface2和interface3，interface4分别代表Vlanif100，Vlanif200，Vlanif300和Vlanif400。
配置注意事项为了提升安全性，推荐部署IS-IS认证功能（参见“配置IS-IS认证”）。其中，以配置IS-IS接口认证为例，详细配置方法请参见“举例：配置IS-IS基本功能”。

配置思路采用如下的思路配置IS-IS的路由聚合：
1. 在各设备上配置IS-IS的基本功能，查看路由表信息。
2. 在DeviceB上配置路由聚合，查看路由表信息。
操作步骤步骤1 配置各路由设备接口IPv4地址。
\# 配置DeviceA。
<HUAWEI> system-view [HUAWEI] sysname DeviceA [DeviceA] vlan batch 100 [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/1 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] ip address 172.16.1.2 255.255.255.0 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] quit DeviceB和DeviceC的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤2 配置IS-IS基本功能。
\# 配置DeviceA。
[DeviceA] isis 1 [DeviceA-isis-1] is-level level-2 [DeviceA-isis-1] network-entity 20.0000.0000.0001.00 [DeviceA-isis-1] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] isis enable 1 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] quit \# 配置DeviceB。
[DeviceB] isis 1 [DeviceB-isis-1] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0002.00 [DeviceB-isis-1] quit [DeviceB] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceB-Vlanif100] isis enable 1 [DeviceB-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceB] interface vlanif 200 [DeviceB-Vlanif200] isis enable 1 [DeviceB-Vlanif200] quit \# 配置DeviceC。
[DeviceC] isis 1 [DeviceC-isis-1] is-level level-1 [DeviceC-isis-1] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0003.00 [DeviceC-isis-1] quit [DeviceC] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceC-Vlanif100] isis enable 1 [DeviceC-Vlanif100] quit DeviceC上的Vlanif200、Vlanif300和Vlanif400的配置与Vlanif100相同，不再赘述。
\# 完成上述配置后，查看设备DeviceA的IS-IS路由表信息。
[DeviceA] display isis route

Route Information for ISIS(1)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- Flags: D-Direct, A-Added to URT, L-Advertised in LSPs, S-IGP Shortcut, U-Up/Down Bit Set Protect Type: L-Link Protect, N-Node Protect ISIS(1) Level-2 Forwarding Table
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- IPV4 Destination IntCost ExtCost ExitInterface NextHop Flags
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
172.16.1.0/24 30 NULL Vlanif100 172.16.4.2 A/-/-/-
172.16.2.0/24 30 NULL Vlanif100 172.16.4.2 A/-/-/-
172.16.3.0/24 30 NULL Vlanif100 172.16.4.2 A/-/-/-
172.16.4.0/24 10 NULL Vlanif100 Direct D/-/L/-
172.17.1.0/24 20 NULL Vlanif100 172.16.4.2 A/-/-/-步骤3 在设备DeviceB上配置路由聚合\# 在DeviceB上将172.16.1.0/24、172.16.2.0/24、172.16.3.0/24聚合成
172.16.0.0/16 。
[DeviceB] isis 1 [DeviceB-isis-1] summary 172.16.0.0 255.255.0.0 level-1-2 [DeviceB-isis-1] quit
----结束检查配置结果\# 查看DeviceA的路由表，可以看到172.16.1.0/24、172.16.2.0/24、172.16.3.0/24聚合成了172.16.0.0/16一条路由。
[DeviceA] display isis route Route Information for ISIS(1)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- Flags: D-Direct, A-Added to URT, L-Advertised in LSPs, S-IGP Shortcut, U-Up/Down Bit Set Protect Type: L-Link Protect, N-Node Protect ISIS(1) Level-2 Forwarding Table
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- IPV4 Destination IntCost ExtCost ExitInterface NextHop Flags
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
172.16.0.0/16 20 NULL Vlanif100 172.16.4.2 A/-/-/-
172.16.4.0/24 10 NULL Vlanif100 Direct D/-/L/-
172.17.1.0/24 20 NULL Vlanif100 172.16.4.2 A/-/-/-配置脚本
● DeviceA \# sysname DeviceA \# vlan batch 100 \# isis 1 is-level level-2 network-entity 20.0000.0000.0001.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 172.17.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1

port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# return
● DeviceB \# sysname DeviceB \# vlan batch 100 200 \# isis 1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0002.00 summary 172.16.0.0 255.255.0.0 level-1-2 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 172.16.4.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif200 ip address 172.17.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# return
● DeviceC \# sysname DeviceC \# vlan batch 100 200 300 400 \# isis 1 is-level level-1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0003.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 172.16.4.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif200 ip address 172.16.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif300 ip address 172.16.2.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif400 ip address 172.16.3.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# interface 10GE1/0/3 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 300

\# interface 10GE1/0/4 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 400 \# return

#### 9.39.8 举例：配置IS-IS引入OSPF环路检测

组网需求在现网应用中，IS-IS路由可以通过路由引入的方式在OSPF进程进行重发布，此类场景通常通过多台设备协同配置路由策略防环，如果引入路由的设备路由策略配置不当，可能导致路由环路。为了避免该问题，可以在IS-IS引入路由时，配置环路检测功能。
如图9-56所示，DeviceA、DeviceB及DeviceF配置IS-IS进程2，DeviceB、DeviceC、DeviceE及DeviceF配置OSPF进程，DeviceC、DeviceD和DeviceE配置IS-IS进程1。
DeviceC配置OSPF引入IS-IS 1，DeviceB配置IS-IS 2引入OSPF，DeviceF配置OSPF引入IS-IS 2。
图 9-56 IS-IS 引入 OSPF 路由环路检测说明本例中interface1，interface2和interface3分别代表Vlanif10，Vlanif20，Vlanif30。
配置注意事项为了提升安全性，推荐部署 IS-IS 认证功能（参见“配置 IS-IS 认证”）。其中，以配置IS-IS接口认证为例，详细配置方法请参见“举例：配置IS-IS基本功能”。
配置思路采用如下思路配置IS-IS引入OSPF环路检测功能：
1. 配置各设备接口的IP地址，实现区域连通性。
2. 在设备上使能IS-IS和OSPF，配置IS-IS和OSPF基本功能。
3. 配置路由引入，构造路由环路。
4. 查看路由表项，验证是否存在路由环路。
5. 使能路由环路检测功能，查看路由表项，验证环路是否解除。

操作步骤步骤1 配置各接口的IP地址以DeviceA、DeviceB、DeviceC和DeviceD为例。
\# 配置DeviceA。
<DeviceA> system-view [DeviceA] vlan batch 10 20 [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/1 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] quit [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/2 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] ip address 10.12.1.1 24 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 20 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] ip address 10.16.1.1 24 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] quit \# 配置DeviceB。
<DeviceB> system-view [DeviceB] vlan batch 10 20 30 [DeviceB] interface 10ge 1/0/1 [DeviceB-10GE1/0/1] port link-type trunk [DeviceB-10GE1/0/1] port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 [DeviceB-10GE1/0/1] quit [DeviceB] interface 10ge 1/0/2 [DeviceB-10GE1/0/2] port link-type trunk [DeviceB-10GE1/0/2] port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 [DeviceB-10GE1/0/2] quit [DeviceB] interface 10ge 1/0/2 [DeviceB-10GE1/0/3] port link-type trunk [DeviceB-10GE1/0/3] port trunk allow-pass vlan 30 [DeviceB-10GE1/0/3] quit [DeviceB] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceB-Vlanif10] ip address 10.12.1.2 24 [DeviceB-Vlanif10] quit [DeviceB] interface vlanif 20 [DeviceB-Vlanif20] ip address 10.26.1.2 24 [DeviceB-Vlanif20] quit [DeviceB] interface vlanif 30 [DeviceB-Vlanif30] ip address 10.23.1.2 24 [DeviceB-Vlanif30] quit \# 配置 DeviceC 。
<DeviceC> system-view [DeviceC] vlan batch 10 20 30 [DeviceC] interface 10ge 1/0/1 [DeviceC-10GE1/0/1] port link-type trunk [DeviceC-10GE1/0/1] port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 [DeviceC-10GE1/0/1] quit [DeviceC] interface 10ge 1/0/2 [DeviceC-10GE1/0/2] port link-type trunk [DeviceC-10GE1/0/2] port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 [DeviceC-10GE1/0/2] quit [DeviceC] interface 10ge 1/0/2 [DeviceC-10GE1/0/3] port link-type trunk [DeviceC-10GE1/0/3] port trunk allow-pass vlan 30 [DeviceC-10GE1/0/3] quit [DeviceC] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceC-Vlanif10] ip address 10.23.1.3 24

[DeviceC-Vlanif10] quit [DeviceC] interface vlanif 20 [DeviceC-Vlanif20] ip address 10.35.1.3 24 [DeviceC-Vlanif20] quit [DeviceC] interface vlanif 30 [DeviceC-Vlanif30] ip address 10.34.1.3 24 [DeviceC-Vlanif30] quit \# 配置DeviceD，DeviceD的Loopback接口下的10.1.1.1/32作为始发路由。
<DeviceD> system-view [DeviceD] interface loopBack 1 [DeviceD-LoopBack1] ip address 10.1.1.1 32 [DeviceD-LoopBack1] isis enable 1 [DeviceD-LoopBack1] quit其他设备配置与上述设备类似，具体配置过程请参考配置脚本。
步骤2 使能IS-IS和OSPF，配置IS-IS和OSPF基本功能及各链路开销，实现域内互通。
\# DeviceA、DeviceB及DeviceF配置IS-IS 2，以DeviceA为例。
[DeviceA] isis 2 [DeviceA-isis-2] cost-style wide [DeviceA-isis-2] network-entity 10.1111.0000.0001.00 [DeviceA-isis-2] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] isis enable 2 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] isis circuit-type p2p [DeviceA-Vlanif10] isis cost 10 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 20 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] isis enable 2 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] isis circuit-type p2p [DeviceA-Vlanif20] isis cost 10 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] quit DeviceB及DeviceF配置与DeviceA类似，具体配置过程请参考配置脚本。
\# DeviceC、DeviceD及DeviceE配置IS-IS 1，以DeviceD为例。
[DeviceD] isis 1 [DeviceD-isis-1] cost-style wide [DeviceD-isis-1] network-entity 10.4444.0000.0001.00 [DeviceD-isis-1] quit [DeviceD] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceD-Vlanif10] isis enable 1 [DeviceD-Vlanif10] isis circuit-type p2p [DeviceD-Vlanif10] isis cost 10 [DeviceD-Vlanif10] quit [DeviceD] interface vlanif 20 [DeviceD-Vlanif20] isis enable 1 [DeviceD-Vlanif20] isis circuit-type p2p [DeviceD-Vlanif20] isis cost 10 [DeviceD-Vlanif20] quit DeviceC及DeviceE配置与DeviceD类似，具体配置过程请参考配置脚本。
\# DeviceB、DeviceC、DeviceE及DeviceF配置OSPF，以DeviceB为例。
[DeviceB] ospf 1 router-id 10.2.2.2 [DeviceB-ospf-1] area 0 [DeviceB-ospf-1-area-0.0.0.0] network 10.23.1.0 0.0.0.255 [DeviceB-ospf-1-area-0.0.0.0] network 10.26.1.0 0.0.0.255 [DeviceB-ospf-1-area-0.0.0.0] quit [DeviceB] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceB-Vlanif10] ospf enable 1 area 0.0.0.0 [DeviceB-Vlanif10] quit [DeviceB] interface vlanif 20

[DeviceB-Vlanif20] ospf enable 1 area 0.0.0.0 [DeviceB-Vlanif20] quit DeviceC、DeviceD及DeviceE配置与DeviceB类似，具体配置过程请参考配置脚本。
步骤3 配置路由引入。
\# 配置DeviceC的OSPF引入IS-IS 1。
[DeviceC] ospf 1 router-id 10.3.3.3 [DeviceC-ospf-1] import-route isis 1 cost 100 type 2 \# 配置DeviceB的IS-IS 2引入OSPF。
[DeviceB] isis 2 [DeviceB-isis-2] import-route ospf 1 \# 配置DeviceF的OSPF引入IS-IS 2。
[DeviceF] ospf 1 router-id 10.6.6.6 [DeviceF-ospf-1] import-route isis 2 cost 10 type 2步骤4 在DeviceB、DeviceE及DeviceF上查看路由表，验证是否存在环路。
\# 查看DeviceB的OSPF邻居信息。
[DeviceB] display ospf peer OSPF Process 1 with Router ID 10.2.2.2 Area 0.0.0.0 interface 10.23.1.2 (Vlanif30)'s neighbors Router ID: 10.3.3.3 Address: 10.23.1.3 State: Full Mode:Nbr is Master Priority: 1 DR: 10.23.1.3 BDR: 10.23.1.2 MTU: 0 Dead timer due in 3 sec Retrans timer interval: 5 Neighbor is up for 28h43m46s Neighbor Up Time : 2021-08-23 09:20:09 Authentication Sequence: [ 0 ] Area 0.0.0.0 interface 10.26.1.2 (Vlanif20)'s neighbors Router ID: 10.6.6.6 Address: 10.26.1.6 State: Full Mode:Nbr is Master Priority: 1 DR: None BDR: None MTU: 0 Dead timer due in 4 sec Retrans timer interval: 5 Neighbor is up for 28h43m46s Neighbor Up Time : 2021-08-23 09:20:09 Authentication Sequence: [ 0 ] \# 查看DeviceB的IS-IS邻居信息。
[DeviceB] display isis peer Peer information for ISIS(2)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- System Id Interface Circuit Id State HoldTime Type PRI
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 6666.0000.0001 Vlanif30 0000000011 Up 29s L1L2 -- 1111.0000.0001 Vlanif20 0000000025 Up 27s L1L2 -- Total Peer(s): 2可以看出，DeviceB与DeviceA、DeviceC及DeviceF建立了连接。
\# 查看DeviceB的路由表。
[DeviceB] display ip routing-table 10.1.1.1 Route Flags: R - relay, D - download to fib, T - to vpn-instance, B - black hole route
------------------------------------------------------------------------------ Routing Table : _public_ Summary Count : 1

Destination/Mask Proto Pre Cost Flags NextHop Interface
10.1.1.1/24 O_ASE 150 1 D 10.26.1.6 Vlanif30可以看出，DeviceB的下一跳为DeviceF。
\# 查看DeviceF的OSPF邻居信息。
[DeviceF] display ospf peer OSPF Process 1 with Router ID 10.6.6.6 Area 0.0.0.0 interface 10.56.1.6 (Vlanif30)'s neighbors Router ID: 10.5.5.5 Address: 10.56.1.5 State: Full Mode:Nbr is Slave Priority: 1 DR: 10.56.1.6 BDR: 10.56.1.5 MTU: 0 Dead timer due in 4 sec Retrans timer interval: 5 Neighbor is up for 28h52m49s Neighbor Up Time : 2021-08-23 09:20:11 Authentication Sequence: [ 0 ] Area 0.0.0.0 interface 10.26.1.6 (Vlanif20)'s neighbors Router ID: 10.2.2.2 Address: 10.26.1.2 State: Full Mode:Nbr is Slave Priority: 1 DR: None BDR: None MTU: 0 Dead timer due in 3 sec Retrans timer interval: 5 Neighbor is up for 28h52m51s Neighbor Up Time : 2021-08-23 09:20:09 Authentication Sequence: [ 0 ] \# 查看DeviceF的IS-IS邻居。
[DeviceF] display isis peer Peer information for ISIS(2)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- System Id Interface Circuit Id State HoldTime Type PRI
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 2222.0000.0001 Vlanif20 0000000011 Up 28s L1L2 -- 1111.0000.0001 Vlanif10 0000000011 Up 25s L1L2 -- Total Peer(s): 2可以看出，DeviceF与DeviceA、DeviceB及DeviceE建立了连接。
\# 查看DeviceF的路由表。
[DeviceF] display ip routing-table 10.1.1.1 Route Flags: R - relay, D - download to fib, T - to vpn-instance, B - black hole route
------------------------------------------------------------------------------ Routing Table : _public_ Summary Count : 1 Destination/Mask Proto Pre Cost Flags NextHop Interface
10.1.1.1/24 ISIS-L2 15 10 D 10.26.1.2 Vlanif20可以看出，DeviceF的下一跳为DeviceB。
此时，在DeviceB和DeviceF处形成环路。
步骤5 在各设备使能路由环路检测功能。
\# 以DeviceA为例。
[DeviceA] route loop-detect isis enable [DeviceA] route loop-detect ospf enable其他设备的配置与DeviceA类似，具体配置过程请参考配置脚本。

步骤6 验证路由环路是否解除。
\# 查看DeviceB的路由表。
[DeviceB] display ip routing-table 10.1.1.1 Route Flags: R - relay, D - download to fib, T - to vpn-instance, B - black hole route
------------------------------------------------------------------------------ Routing Table : _public_ Summary Count : 1 Destination/Mask Proto Pre Cost Flags NextHop Interface
10.1.1.1/24 O_ASE 150 100 D 10.23.1.3 Vlanif30可以看出，DeviceB的下一跳为DeviceC。
\# 查看DeviceF的路由表。
[DeviceF] display ip routing-table 10.1.1.1 Route Flags: R - relay, D - download to fib, T - to vpn-instance, B - black hole route
------------------------------------------------------------------------------ Routing Table : _public_ Summary Count : 1 Destination/Mask Proto Pre Cost Flags NextHop Interface
10.1.1.1/24 ISIS-L2 15 10 D 10.26.1.2 Vlanif20可以看出，DeviceF的下一跳为DeviceB，DeviceB不再优选DeviceF。此时，在DeviceB和DeviceF上已解除环路。
----结束配置脚本
● DeviceA \# sysname DeviceA \# vlan batch 10 20 \# isis 2 cost-style wide network-entity 10.1111.0000.0001.00 \# route loop-detect isis enable \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 10.12.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 2 isis circuit-type p2p isis cost 10 \# interface Vlanif20 ip address 10.16.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 2 isis circuit-type p2p isis cost 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 \# route loop-detect ospf enable

\# return

#### 9.39.9 举例：配置静态BFD for IS-IS

组网需求如图9-57所示，DeviceA和DeviceB之间采用二层交设备相连。DeviceA、DeviceB和DeviceC 之间运行 IS-IS 。使用 BFD 检测 DeviceA 和 DeviceB 的 IS-IS 邻居关系。当 DeviceA和DeviceB之间的链路发生故障时，BFD能够快速检测到故障并通告给IS-IS协议。
图 9-57 配置静态 BFD for IS-IS 组网图说明本例中interface1，interface2分别代表Vlanif100，Vlanif200。

配置注意事项为了提升安全性，推荐部署IS-IS认证功能（参见“配置IS-IS认证”）。其中，以配置IS-IS接口认证为例，详细配置方法请参见“举例：配置IS-IS基本功能”。
配置思路采用如下思路配置BFD for IS-IS特性：
1. 在各设备上使能IS-IS基本功能。
2. 在DeviceA和DeviceB上使能BFD检测机制。
操作步骤步骤1 配置各设备接口的IPv4地址。
\# 配置DeviceA。
<HUAWEI> system-view [HUAWEI] sysname DeviceA [DeviceA] vlan batch 100 [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/1 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] ip address 10.1.1.1 255.255.255.0 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] quit DeviceB和DeviceC的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤2 配置IS-IS基本功能。
\# 配置DeviceA。
[DeviceA] isis 1 [DeviceA-isis-1] is-level level-2 [DeviceA-isis-1] network-entity aa.1111.1111.1111.00 [DeviceA-isis-1] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] isis enable 1 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] quit DeviceB和DeviceC的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
配置完成后，可以看到DeviceA与DeviceB建立了邻居关系。
[DeviceA] display isis peer Peer information for ISIS(1)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- System ID Interface Circuit ID State HoldTime Type PRI
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 2222.2222.2222 Vlanif100 2222.2222.2222.00 Up 23s L2 64 Total Peer(s): 1 DeviceA的IS-IS路由表中有去往DeviceB和DeviceC的表项。
[DeviceA] display isis route Route Information for ISIS(1)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Flags: D-Direct, A-Added to URT, L-Advertised in LSPs, S-IGP Shortcut, U-Up/Down Bit Set Protect Type: L-Link Protect, N-Node Protect ISIS(1) Level-1 Forwarding Table
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- IPV4 Destination IntCost ExtCost ExitInterface NextHop Flags
-------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.1.1.0/24 10 NULL Vlanif100 Direct D/-/L/-
10.2.1.0/24 20 NULL Vlanif100 10.1.1.2 A/-/-/-步骤3 配置静态BFD for IS-IS。
\# 在DeviceA上使能BFD，并配置BFD Session。
[DeviceA] bfd [DeviceA-bfd] quit [DeviceA] bfd atob bind peer-ip 10.1.1.2 interface vlanif 100 [DeviceA-bfd-session-atob] discriminator local 1 [DeviceA-bfd-session-atob] discriminator remote 2 [DeviceA-bfd-session-atob] quit DeviceB的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
配置完成后，在 DeviceA 上执行 display bfd session 命令，可以看到 BFD Session 的状态为Up。
[DeviceA] display bfd session all S: Static session D: Dynamic session IP: IP session IF: Single-hop session PEER: Multi-hop session LDP: LDP session LSP: Label switched path AUTO: Automatically negotiated session VXLAN: VXLAN session TNL: Tunnel (w): State in WTR (*): State is invalid Total UP/DOWN Session Number : 1/0
------------------------------------------------------------------------ Local Remote PeerIpAddr State Type Interface Name
------------------------------------------------------------------------ 1 2 10.1.1.2 Up S_IP_IF Vlanif100步骤4 使能IS-IS的快速感知功能。
\# 配置DeviceA。
[DeviceA] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] isis bfd static [DeviceA-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceA] quit DeviceB的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
----结束检查配置结果\# 在DeviceA上打开调试信息开关。
<DeviceA> debugging isis adjacency 1 interface vlanif 100 <DeviceA> debugging isis circuit-information 1 interface vlanif 100 <DeviceA> terminal debugging <DeviceA> terminal monitor

\# 对DeviceB的Vlanif100接口执行shutdown命令，模拟链路故障。
[DeviceB] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceB-Vlanif100] shutdown \# 在DeviceA上可以看到以下日志信息。
ISIS adjacency state change. (SysInstance=1, SysLevel=1, CircI ndex=2, CircIfIndex=20, LspId=2222.2222.2222.00.00, AdjState=1, IfIndex=20, IfNa me=Vlanif100, Reason=BFD detected that the neighbor went Down, SubReason=The BFD session went Down)
表明IS-IS根据BFD报告的故障删除了与DeviceB的邻居关系。
配置脚本
● DeviceA \# sysname DeviceA \# vlan batch 100 \# bfd \# isis 1 is-level level-2 network-entity aa.1111.1111.1111.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 10.1.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 isis bfd static \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# bfd atob bind peer-ip 10.1.1.2 interface Vlanif100 discriminator local 1 discriminator remote 2 \# return
● DeviceB \# sysname DeviceB \# vlan batch 100 200 \# bfd \# isis 1 is-level level-2 network-entity aa.2222.2222.2222.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 10.1.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 isis bfd static \# interface Vlanif200 ip address 10.2.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \#

interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# bfd btoa bind peer-ip 10.1.1.1 interface Vlanif100 discriminator local 2 discriminator remote 1 \# return
● DeviceC \# sysname DeviceC \# vlan batch 100 \# isis 1 is-level level-2 network-entity aa.3333.3333.3333.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 10.2.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# return

#### 9.39.10 举例：配置动态BFD for IS-IS

组网需求如图9-58所示，DeviceA、DeviceB和DeviceC之间运行IS-IS协议。使能DeviceA、DeviceB和DeviceC的IS-IS进程BFD特性。业务流量在主链路DeviceA→DeviceB上传送，链路DeviceA→DeviceC→DeviceB作为备份链路。在DeviceA和DeviceB之间的链路上创建接口的BFD特性，当DeviceA和DeviceB之间的链路出现故障时，BFD能够快速检测到故障并通告给IS-IS协议，使业务流量使用备份链路传送。
图 9-58 配置动态 BFD for IS-IS 组网图说明本例中interface1，interface2和interface3分别代表Vlanif100，Vlanif200，Vlanif300。

配置注意事项为了提升安全性，推荐部署IS-IS认证功能（参见“配置IS-IS认证”）。其中，以配置IS-IS接口认证为例，详细配置方法请参见“举例：配置IS-IS基本功能”。
配置思路采用如下思路配置BFD for IS-IS特性：
1. 在各设备上使能IS-IS基本功能，保证各路由的连通。
2. 配置IS-IS接口开销值控制路由的选路功能。
3. 使能全局BFD特性。
4. 在DeviceA、DeviceB和DeviceC上使能IS-IS进程的BFD检测机制。
5. 在DeviceA和DeviceB上使能接口的BFD检测机制。
操作步骤步骤1 配置各设备接口的IPv4地址。
\# 配置DeviceA。
<HUAWEI> system-view [HUAWEI] sysname DeviceA [DeviceA] vlan batch 100 200 [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/1 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] quit [DeviceA-vlan100] quit [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/2 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] ip address 10.1.1.1 255.255.255.0 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 200 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] ip address 10.1.3.1 255.255.255.0 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] quit DeviceB和DeviceC的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤2 配置IS-IS基本功能。
\# 配置DeviceA。
[DeviceA] isis [DeviceA-isis-1] is-level level-2 [DeviceA-isis-1] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 [DeviceA-isis-1] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] isis enable 1 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] isis peer hold-max-cost timer 100000 [DeviceA-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 200 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] isis enable 1 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] isis peer hold-max-cost timer 100000 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] quit DeviceB和DeviceC的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。

\# 配置完成后，使用display isis peer命令，可以查看DeviceA和DeviceB、DeviceA和DeviceC建立了邻居关系。以DeviceA为例。
[DeviceA] display isis peer Peer information for ISIS(1)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- System Id Interface Circuit Id State HoldTime Type PRI
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000.0000.0002 Vlanif100 0000.0000.0002.01 Up 9s L2 64 0000.0000.0003 Vlanif200 0000.0000.0001.02 Up 21s L2 64 Total Peer(s): 2 \# 设备之间已经互相学到路由。以查看DeviceA的路由表为例。
[DeviceA] display ip routing-table Route Flags: R - relay, D - download to fib, T - to vpn-instance, B - black hole route
------------------------------------------------------------------------------ Routing Table: _public_ Destinations : 8 Routes : 9 Destination/Mask Proto Pre Cost Flags NextHop Interface
10.1.1.0/24 Direct 0 0 D 10.1.1.1 Vlanif100
10.1.1.1/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0
10.1.2.0/24 ISIS 15 20 D 10.1.1.2 Vlanif100
10.1.3.0/24 Direct 0 0 D 10.1.3.1 Vlanif200
10.1.3.1/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0
127.0.0.0/8 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0
127.0.0.1/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0
172.16.1.0/24 ISIS 15 20 D 10.1.3.2 Vlanif200从路由表可以看出，去往172.16.1.0/24的路由下一跳地址为10.1.3.2，流量在主链路DeviceA→DeviceB上传输。
步骤3 配置接口开销值。
\# 配置DeviceA。
[DeviceA] interface Vlanif200 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] isis cost 5 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] quit DeviceB的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤4 配置IS-IS进程的BFD特性。
\# 在DeviceA上使能IS-IS的BFD特性。
[DeviceA] bfd [DeviceA-bfd] quit [DeviceA] isis [DeviceA-isis-1] bfd all-interfaces enable [DeviceA-isis-1] quit DeviceB和DeviceC的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
\# 配置完成后，在DeviceA、DeviceB或DeviceC上执行display isis bfd session all命令，可以看到BFD State的状态为Up。以DeviceA的显示为例。
[DeviceA] display isis bfd session all BFD session information for ISIS(1)
----------------------------------------------------------------- Peer System ID : 0000.0000.0002 Interface : Vlanif200 TX : 10 BFD State : up Peer IP Address : 10.1.3.2 RX : 10 LocDis : 16385 Local IP Address: 10.1.3.1 Multiplier : 3 RemDis : 16388 Type : L2 Diag : No diagnostic information

Peer System ID : 0000.0000.0003 Interface : Vlanif100 TX : 10 BFD State : up Peer IP Address : 10.1.1.2 RX : 10 LocDis : 16386 Local IP Address: 10.1.1.1 Multiplier : 3 RemDis : 16387 Type : L2 Diag : No diagnostic information Total BFD session(s): 2从上面信息可以看出，DeviceA与DeviceB、DeviceC的BFD会话状态为Up。
步骤5 配置接口的BFD特性。
\# 在DeviceA的Vlanif200接口上配置BFD特性，并指定最小发送和接收间隔为100ms本地检测时间倍数为4。
[DeviceA] interface Vlanif200 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] isis bfd enable [DeviceA-Vlanif200] isis bfd min-tx-interval 100 min-rx-interval 100 detect-multiplier 4 [DeviceA-Vlanif200] quit \# 在DeviceB的Vlanif200接口上配置BFD特性，并指定最小发送和接收间隔为100ms本地检测时间倍数为4。
[DeviceB] interface Vlanif200 [DeviceB-Vlanif200] isis bfd enable [DeviceB-Vlanif200] isis bfd min-tx-interval 100 min-rx-interval 100 detect-multiplier 4 [DeviceB-Vlanif200] quit
----结束检查配置结果\# 配置完成后，在DeviceB上执行display isis bfd session all命令，可以看到BFD参数已生效。
[DeviceB] display isis bfd session all BFD session information for ISIS(1)
----------------------------------------------------------------- Peer System ID : 0000.0000.0001 Interface : Vlanif200 TX : 100 BFD State : up Peer IP Address : 10.1.3.1 RX : 100 LocDis : 16385 Local IP Address: 10.1.3.2 Multiplier : 4 RemDis : 16385 Type : L2 Diag : No diagnostic information Peer System ID : 0000.0000.0003 Interface : Vlanif100 TX : 10 BFD State : up Peer IP Address : 10.1.2.1 RX : 10 LocDis : 16385 Local IP Address: 10.1.2.2 Multiplier : 4 RemDis : 16385 Type : L2 Diag : No diagnostic information Total BFD session (s):2 \# 对DeviceB的Vlanif200接口执行shutdown命令，模拟主链路故障。
[DeviceB] interface Vlanif200 [DeviceB-Vlanif200] shutdown \# 在DeviceA上，查看IP路由表。
[DeviceA] display ip routing-table Route Flags: R - relay, D - download to fib, T - to vpn-instance, B - black hole route
------------------------------------------------------------------------------ Routing Table : _public_ Destinations : 9 Routes : 9 Destination/Mask Proto Pre Cost Flags NextHop Interface
10.1.1.0/24 Direct 0 0 D 10.1.1.1 Vlanif100
10.1.1.1/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 Vlanif100

10.1.1.255/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 Vlanif100
10.1.2.0/24 ISIS 15 20 D 10.1.1.2 Vlanif100
127.0.0.0/8 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0
127.0.0.1/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0
127.255.255.255/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0
172.16.1.0/24 ISIS 15 30 D 10.1.1.2 Vlanif100
255.255.255.255/32 Direct 0 0 D 127.0.0.1 InLoopBack0
从路由表可以看出，在主链路失效后，备份链路DeviceA-DeviceC-DeviceB生效，去往
172.16.1.0/24的路由下一跳地址为10.1.1.2。
\# 在DeviceA上执行display isis bfd session all命令，只能看到DeviceA和DeviceC之
间的BFD State的状态为Up。
[DeviceA] display isis bfd session all
BFD session information for ISIS(1)
-----------------------------------------------------------------
Peer System ID : 0000.0000.0003 Interface : Vlanif100
TX : 10 BFD State : up Peer IP Address : 10.1.1.2
RX : 10 LocDis : 16385 Local IP Address: 10.1.1.1
Multiplier : 3 RemDis : 16388 Type : L2

配置脚本
● DeviceA
\#
sysname DeviceA
\#
vlan batch 100 200
\#
bfd
\#
isis 1
is-level level-2
bfd all-interfaces enable
network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00
\#
interface Vlanif100
ip address 10.1.1.1 255.255.255.0
isis enable 1
isis peer hold-max-cost timer 100000
\#
interface Vlanif200
ip address 10.1.3.1 255.255.255.0
isis enable 1
isis peer hold-max-cost timer 100000
isis cost 5
isis bfd enable
isis bfd min-tx-interval 100 min-rx-interval 100 detect-multiplier 4
\#
interface 10GE1/0/1
port link-type trunk
port trunk allow-pass vlan 100
\#
interface 10GE1/0/2
port link-type trunk
port trunk allow-pass vlan 200
\#
return
● DeviceB
\#
sysname DeviceB
\#
vlan batch 100 200 300
\#

bfd \# isis 1 is-level level-2 bfd all-interfaces enable network-entity 10.0000.0000.0002.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 10.1.2.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif200 ip address 10.1.3.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 isis cost 5 isis bfd enable isis bfd min-tx-interval 100 min-rx-interval 100 detect-multiplier 4 \# interface Vlanif300 ip address 172.16.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# interface 10GE1/0/3 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 300 \# return
● DeviceC \# sysname DeviceC \# vlan batch 100 200 \# bfd \# isis 1 is-level level-2 bfd all-interfaces enable network-entity 10.0000.0000.0003.00 \# interface Vlanif100 ip address 10.1.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif200 ip address 10.1.2.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# return

#### 9.39.11 举例：配置动态BFD for IPv6 IS-IS

组网需求如图9-59所示，DeviceS到DeviceD之间的流量有两条路径，正常情况下流量走主路径，当主路径的链路故障或者Switch故障时，通过配置BFD for IPv6 IS-IS，使DeviceS和DeviceD之间的流量快速切换到备份路径上。
图 9-59 动态 BFD for IPv6 IS-IS 组网示意图说明本例中interface1，interface2和interface3分别代表Vlanif100，Vlanif200，Vlanif300。
配置思路采用如下思路配置动态BFD for IPv6 IS-IS特性：
1. 配置设备各接口的IPv6地址。
2. 在各设备上配置IPv6 IS-IS基本功能，保证各路由的IPv6连通性。
3. 在各设备上调整接口的IS-IS cost值，保证主路径优先级高与备路径优先级。
4. 在各设备上配置全局使能BFD。
5. 在各设备的IS-IS视图下使能BFD for IPv6 IS-IS。
操作步骤步骤1 配置各接口的IPv6地址。
\# 以DeviceS为例，其他设备的配置类似，此不赘述。
<HUAWEI> system-view [HUAWEI] sysname DeviceS [DeviceS] vlan batch 100 200 [DeviceS] interface 10ge 1/0/1 [DeviceS-10GE1/0/1] port link-type trunk [DeviceS-10GE1/0/1] port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 [DeviceS-10GE1/0/1] quit [DeviceS] interface 10ge 1/0/2 [DeviceS-10GE1/0/2] port link-type trunk [DeviceS-10GE1/0/2] port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 [DeviceS-10GE1/0/2] quit [DeviceS] interface vlanif 100

[DeviceS-Vlanif100] ipv6 enable [DeviceS-Vlanif100] ipv6 address 2001:db8:1::1/64 [DeviceS-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceS] interface vlanif 200 [DeviceS-Vlanif200] ipv6 enable [DeviceS-Vlanif200] ipv6 address 2001:db8:2::1/64 [DeviceS-Vlanif200] quit步骤2 配置IPv6 IS-IS的基本功能。
\# 配置DeviceS。
[DeviceS] isis 10 [DeviceS-isis-10] is-level level-2 [DeviceS-isis-10] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 [DeviceS-isis-10] ipv6 enable [DeviceS-isis-10] quit [DeviceS] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceS-Vlanif100] isis ipv6 enable 10 [DeviceS-Vlanif100] isis peer hold-max-cost timer 100000 [DeviceS-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceS] interface vlanif 200 [DeviceS-Vlanif200] isis ipv6 enable 10 [DeviceS-Vlanif200] isis peer hold-max-cost timer 100000 [DeviceS-Vlanif200] quit \# 配置DeviceN。
[DeviceN] isis 10 [DeviceN-isis-10] is-level level-2 [DeviceN-isis-10] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0002.00 [DeviceN-isis-10] ipv6 enable [DeviceN-isis-10] quit [DeviceN] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceN-Vlanif100] isis ipv6 enable 10 [DeviceN-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceN] interface vlanif 200 [DeviceN-Vlanif200] isis ipv6 enable 10 [DeviceN-Vlanif200] quit \# 配置DeviceD。
[DeviceD] isis 10 [DeviceD-isis-10] is-level level-2 [DeviceD-isis-10] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0003.00 [DeviceD-isis-10] ipv6 enable [DeviceD-isis-10] quit [DeviceD] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceD-Vlanif100] isis ipv6 enable 10 [DeviceD-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceD] interface vlanif 200 [DeviceD-Vlanif200] isis ipv6 enable 10 [DeviceD-Vlanif200] quit [DeviceD] interface vlanif 300 [DeviceD-Vlanif300] isis ipv6 enable 10 [DeviceD-Vlanif300] quit完成此步骤后，执行命令display ipv6 routing-table，可发现各设备之间互相学习到对方的IPv6路由。
步骤3 配置接口的IS-IS cost。
\# 配置DeviceS。
[DeviceS] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceS-Vlanif100] isis ipv6 cost 1 level-2 [DeviceS-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceS] interface vlanif 200 [DeviceS-Vlanif200] isis ipv6 cost 10 level-2 [DeviceS-Vlanif200] quit

\# 配置DeviceN。
[DeviceN] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceN-Vlanif100] isis ipv6 cost 10 level-2 [DeviceN-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceN] interface vlanif 200 [DeviceN-Vlanif200] isis ipv6 cost 10 level-2 [DeviceN-Vlanif200] quit \# 配置DeviceD。
[DeviceD] interface vlanif 100 [DeviceD-Vlanif100] isis ipv6 cost 1 level-2 [DeviceD-Vlanif100] quit [DeviceD] interface vlanif 200 [DeviceD-Vlanif200] isis ipv6 cost 10 level-2 [DeviceD-Vlanif200] quit步骤4 配置BFD for IPv6 IS-IS。
\# 配置DeviceS。
[DeviceS] bfd [DeviceS-bfd] quit [DeviceS] isis 10 [DeviceS-isis-10] ipv6 bfd all-interfaces enable [DeviceS-isis-10] ipv6 bfd all-interfaces min-tx-interval 150 min-rx-interval 150 [DeviceS-isis-10] quit \# 配置DeviceN。
[DeviceN] bfd [DeviceN-bfd] quit [DeviceN] isis 10 [DeviceN-isis-10] ipv6 bfd all-interfaces enable [DeviceN-isis-10] ipv6 bfd all-interfaces min-tx-interval 150 min-rx-interval 150 [DeviceN-isis-10] quit \# 配置DeviceD。
[DeviceD] bfd [DeviceD-bfd] quit [DeviceD] isis 10 [DeviceD-isis-10] ipv6 bfd all-interfaces enable [DeviceD-isis-10] ipv6 bfd all-interfaces min-tx-interval 150 min-rx-interval 150 [DeviceD-isis-10] quit \# 配置完成后，在DeviceS或DeviceD上执行display isis ipv6 bfd session all命令，可以看到BFD参数已生效。以DeviceS的显示为例。
[DeviceS] display isis ipv6 bfd session all Peer System ID : 0000.0000.0003 Type : L2 Interface : Vlanif100 IPv6 BFD State : up TX : 150 RX : 150 Multiplier : 3 LocDis : 16386 Local IPv6 Address: FE80::E0:2F47:B103:1 RemDis : 16386 Peer IPv6 Address : FE80::E0:2F47:B107:1 Diag : No diagnostic information Peer System ID : 0000.0000.0002 Type : L2 Interface : Vlanif200 IPv6 BFD State : up TX : 150 RX : 150 Multiplier : 3 LocDis : 16386 Local IPv6 Address: FE80::C964:0:B203:1 RemDis : 16386 Peer IPv6 Address : FE80::C964:0:B8B6:1 Diag : No diagnostic information Total BFD session(s): 2
----结束

检查配置结果\# 在设备DeviceS上执行display ipv6 routing-table 2001:db8:4::1 64命令，查看IPv6路由表。可以看到下一跳地址为FE80::E0:2F47:B107:1，出接口为Vlanif100。
[DeviceS] display ipv6 routing-table 2001:db8:4::1 64 Routing Table : public Summary Count : 1 Destination : 2001:db8:4:: PrefixLength : 64 NextHop : FE80::E0:2F47:B107:1 Preference : 15 Cost : 11 Protocol : ISIS RelayNextHop : :: TunnelID : 0x0 Interface : Vlanif100 Flags : D \# 对DeviceD的Vlanif100接口执行shutdown命令，模拟主链路故障。
[DeviceD] interface Vlanif100 [DeviceD-Vlanif100] shutdown \# 在设备DeviceS上执行display ipv6 routing-table 2001:db8:4::1 64命令，查看IPv6路由表。
[DeviceS] display ipv6 routing-table 2001:db8:4::1 64 Routing Table : public Summary Count : 1 Destination : 2001:db8:4:: PrefixLength : 64 NextHop : FE80::C964:0:B8B6:1 Preference : 15 Cost : 20 Protocol : ISIS RelayNextHop : :: TunnelID : 0x0 Interface :Vlanif200 Flags : D从路由表可以看出，在主链路失效后，备份路径生效，去往2001:db8:4::/64的路由下一跳地址变为FE80::C964:0:B8B6:1，出接口变为Vlanif200，路由开销也发生变化。
\# 在DeviceS上执行display isis ipv6 bfd session all命令，可看到DeviceS和DeviceN之间的BFD会话只有1条状态为Up。
[DeviceS] display isis ipv6 bfd 10 session all Peer System ID : 0000.0000.0002 Type : L2 Interface : Vlanif200 IPv6 BFD State : up TX : 150 RX : 150 Multiplier : 3 LocDis : 16386 Local IPv6 Address: FE80::C964:0:B203:1 RemDis : 16386 Peer IPv6 Address : FE80::C964:0:B8B6:1 Diag : No diagnostic information Total BFD session(s): 1配置脚本
● DeviceS \# sysname DeviceS \# vlan batch 100 200 \# bfd \# isis 10 is-level level-2 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 \# ipv6 enable topology ipv6 ipv6 bfd all-interfaces enable ipv6 bfd all-interfaces min-tx-interval 150 min-rx-interval 150 \#

\# interface Vlanif100 ipv6 enable ipv6 address 2001:db8:1::1/64 isis ipv6 enable 10 isis ipv6 cost 1 level-2 isis peer hold-max-cost timer 100000 \# interface Vlanif200 ipv6 enable ipv6 address 2001:db8:2::1/64 isis ipv6 enable 10 isis ipv6 cost 10 level-2 isis peer hold-max-cost timer 100000 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# return
● DeviceN \# sysname DeviceN \# vlan batch 100 200 \# bfd \# isis 10 is-level level-2 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0002.00 \# ipv6 enable topology ipv6 ipv6 bfd all-interfaces enable ipv6 bfd all-interfaces min-tx-interval 150 min-rx-interval 150 \# \# interface Vlanif100 ipv6 enable ipv6 address 2001:db8:2::2/64 isis ipv6 enable 10 isis ipv6 cost 10 level-2 \# interface Vlanif200 ipv6 enable ipv6 address 2001:db8:3::1/64 isis ipv6 enable 10 isis ipv6 cost 10 level-2 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# return
● DeviceD \# sysname DeviceD \# vlan batch 100 200 300 \#

bfd \# isis 10 is-level level-2 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0003.00 \# ipv6 enable topology ipv6 ipv6 bfd all-interfaces enable ipv6 bfd all-interfaces min-tx-interval 150 min-rx-interval 150 \# \# interface Vlanif100 ipv6 enable ipv6 address 2001:db8:1::2/64 isis ipv6 enable 10 isis ipv6 cost 1 level-2 \# interface Vlanif200 ipv6 enable ipv6 address 2001:db8:3::2/64 isis ipv6 enable 10 isis ipv6 cost 10 level-2 \# interface Vlanif300 ipv6 enable ipv6 address 2001:db8:4::1/64 isis ipv6 enable 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 100 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 200 \# interface 10GE1/0/3 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 300 \# return

#### 9.39.12 举例：配置IS-IS Auto FRR

组网需求当网络出现故障时，IS-IS Auto FRR可以将流量快速切换到备份链路上，而不用等待路由收敛，从而达到流量不中断的目的。
如图 9-60 所示，四台设备之间运行 IS-IS 协议，都是 Level-1-2 路由设备，当 DeviceC 或DeviceA与DeviceC之前链路出现故障时，DeviceA转发的流量快速切换到备份链路上。
图 9-60 配置 IS-IS Auto FRR 组网图说明本例中interface1，interface2和interface3分别代表Vlanif10，Vlanif20，Vlanif30。

配置注意事项为了提升安全性，推荐部署IS-IS认证功能（参见“配置IS-IS认证”）。其中，以配置IS-IS接口认证为例，详细配置方法请参见“举例：配置IS-IS基本功能”。
配置思路采用如下思路配置IS-IS Auto FRR：
1. 配置各路由设备接口IPv4地址。
2. 在各路由设备上使能IS-IS基本功能。
3. 在DeviceA的Vlanif20接口上配置较大开销（需满足IS-IS Auto FRR流量保护不等式），使流量优选链路T。
4. 由于需保护的是DeviceA转发的流量，因此在DeviceA上使能IS-IS Auto FRR。
操作步骤步骤 1 配置各路由设备接口 IPv4 地址。
\# 配置DeviceA。
<HUAWEI> system-view [HUAWEI] sysname DeviceA [DeviceA] vlan batch 10 20 [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/1 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/1] quit [DeviceA] interface 10ge 1/0/2 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] port link-type trunk [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 [DeviceA-10GE1/0/2] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] ip address 10.0.1.1 255.255.255.0 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] quit

[DeviceA] interface vlanif 20 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] ip address 10.1.1.1 255.255.255.0 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] quit DeviceB，DeviceC和DeviceD的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤2 在所有设备上使能IS-IS，指定网络实体。
\# 配置DeviceA。
[DeviceA] isis 1 [DeviceA-isis-1] is-level level-1-2 [DeviceA-isis-1] network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 [DeviceA-isis-1] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 10 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] isis enable 1 [DeviceA-Vlanif10] quit [DeviceA] interface vlanif 20 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] isis enable 1 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] quit DeviceB，DeviceC和DeviceD的配置过程与DeviceA类似，在此不再赘述，具体请参考配置脚本。
步骤3 配置DeviceA的Vlanif20接口开销为30，并查看路由信息。
\# 配置DeviceA的Vlanif20接口开销为30。
[DeviceA] interface vlanif 20 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] isis cost 30 [DeviceA-Vlanif20] quit \# 查看DeviceA到DeviceD的链路信息。由于链路T的开销最小，因此，IS-IS优选链路T来传送DeviceA转发的流量。
[DeviceA] display isis route 10.4.1.0 verbose Route Information for ISIS(1)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- Flags: D-Direct, A-Added to URT, L-Advertised in LSPs, S-IGP Shortcut, U-Up/Down Bit Set Protect Type: L-Link Protect, N-Node Protect ISIS(1) Level-1 Forwarding Table
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- IPV4 Dest : 10.4.1.0/24 Int. Cost : 30 Ext. Cost : NULL Admin Tag : - Src Count : 1 Flags : A/-/L/- Priority : Low Age : 00:09:34 NextHop : Interface : ExitIndex :
10.0.1.2 Vlanif20 0x00000007 ISIS(1) Level-2 Forwarding Table
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- IPV4 Dest : 10.4.1.0/24 Int. Cost : 30 Ext. Cost : NULL Admin Tag : - Src Count : 2 Flags : -/-/-/- Priority : Low Age : 00:00:00 NextHop : Interface : ExitIndex :
步骤4 使能IS-IS Auto FRR。
\# 在 DeviceA 上使能 IS-IS Auto FRR 。
[DeviceA] isis [DeviceA-isis-1] frr [DeviceA-isis-1-frr] loop-free-alternate

[DeviceA-isis-1-frr] quit [DeviceA-isis-1] quit
----结束检查配置结果\# 查看DeviceA到DeviceD的路由信息。
[DeviceA] display isis route 10.4.1.0 verbose Route Information for ISIS(1)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- Flags: D-Direct, A-Added to URT, L-Advertised in LSPs, S-IGP Shortcut, U-Up/Down Bit Set Protect Type: L-Link Protect, N-Node Protect ISIS(1) Level-1 Forwarding Table
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- IPV4 Dest : 10.4.1.0/24 Int. Cost : 30 Ext. Cost : NULL Admin Tag : - Src Count : 1 Flags : A/-/L/- Priority : Low Age : 00:00:25 NextHop : Interface : ExitIndex :
10.0.1.2 Vlanif10 0x00000007 (B)10.1.1.2 Vlanif20 0x00000008 ISIS(1) Level-2 Forwarding Table
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- IPV4 Dest : 10.4.1.0/24 Int. Cost : 30 Ext. Cost : NULL Admin Tag : - Src Count : 3 Flags : -/-/-/- Priority : Low Age : 00:00:00 NextHop : Interface : ExitIndex : -由于使能了IS-IS FRR，IS-IS生成了一条备份路由。
Auto配置脚本
● DeviceA \# sysname DeviceA \# vlan batch 10 20 \# isis 1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0001.00 frr loop-free-alternate level-1 loop-free-alternate level-2 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 10.0.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif20 ip address 10.1.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 isis cost 30 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 20

\# return
● DeviceB \# sysname DeviceB \# vlan batch 10 20 \# isis 1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0002.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 10.1.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif20 ip address 10.2.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 \# return
● DeviceC \# sysname DeviceC \# vlan batch 10 20 \# isis 1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0003.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 10.0.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif20 ip address 10.3.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 \# return
● DeviceD \# sysname DeviceD \# vlan batch 10 20 30 \# isis 1 network-entity 10.0000.0000.0004.00 \# interface Vlanif10 ip address 10.3.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1

\# interface Vlanif20 ip address 10.2.1.2 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface Vlanif30 ip address 10.4.1.1 255.255.255.0 isis enable 1 \# interface 10GE1/0/1 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 10 \# interface 10GE1/0/2 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 20 \# interface 10GE1/0/3 port link-type trunk port trunk allow-pass vlan 30 \# return

### 9.40 IS-IS常见配置错误

#### 9.40.1 IS-IS邻居建立不成功

故障现象在链路正常的情况下，IS-IS邻居建立不成功。
可能原因
1. 链路两端的设备的IS-IS Level不匹配
2. 链路两端设备的区域地址不匹配
3. 链路两端设备的认证方式不匹配操作步骤步骤1 检查链路两端的设备的IS-IS Level是否匹配
● 执行命令display current-configuration configuration isis | include is-level，可以查看两端 IS-IS 进程的 Level 配置。
● 执行命令display current-configuration interface interface-type interface- number | include isis circuit-level，可以查看接口的IS-IS Level配置。
需要保证链路两端的Level匹配才能建立起IS-IS邻居。

说明如果使用命令display current-configuration interface interface-type interface-number | include isis circuit-level无法查询到接口的Level级别，则表示该接口级别为缺省值。该缺省值可以使用命令display default-parameter isis查看字段Circuit-Level。
接口Level匹配的原则如下：
● 如果本端接口Level级别为Level-1，则对端接口Level级别必须为Level-1或Level-1-2。
● 如果本端接口Level级别为Level-2，则对端接口Level级别必须为Level-2或Level-1-2。
● 如果本端接口Level级别为Level-1-2，则对端接口Level级别可以为Level-1、Level-2或Level-1-2。
如果链路两端Level不匹配，请执行以下命令修改Level级别：
● 在IS-IS视图下使用命令is-level修改设备全局的IS-IS Level级别。
● 在接口视图下使用命令isis circuit-level修改设备接口的IS-IS Level级别。
步骤2 检查链路两端设备的区域地址是否匹配执行命令display current-configuration configuration isis，可以查看设备的区域地址信息。
说明如果链路两端建立Level-1邻居，需要保证链路两端设备在同一个区域内。
一个IS-IS进程最多可以配置3个区域地址，两端只要有一个区域地址相同，即可建立Level-1邻居。
建立IS-IS Level-2邻居时，不需要判断区域地址是否匹配。
如果链路两端无相同区域地址，请在IS-IS视图下使用命令network-entity修改设备的区域地址。
步骤3 检查链路两端设备的认证方式是否匹配执行display current-configuration interface interface-type interface-number | include isis authentication-mode命令查看两端接口的IS-IS认证配置。
如果两端认证方式不匹配，请在链路两端的IS-IS接口视图下执行命令isis authentication-mode，将链路两端设置为相同的认证类型及相同的认证密码。
----结束

#### 9.40.2 设备学习不到IS-IS路由

故障现象在链路正常的情况下，设备学习不到IS-IS路由。
可能原因邻居建立失败
1. IS-IS
2. IS-IS开销类型不匹配
3. IS-IS路由不是最优路由

操作步骤步骤1 检查IS-IS邻居是否正常建立在路径上的每一台设备上执行命令display isis peer，查看IS-IS邻居是否都正常建立。
如果IS-IS邻居不成功，请参见9.40.1 IS-IS邻居建立不成功。
步骤2 检查IS-IS开销类型是否匹配分别在发布路由的设备和学习不到路由的设备上，执行display current- configuration configuration isis命令，查看IS-IS的开销类型配置。
只有开销类型匹配，才能学到路由。
IS-IS的开销类型可以配置为以下5种模式：
● narrow：接收和发送开销值类型为narrow的报文。
● narrow-compatible：可以接收开销值类型为narrow和wide的报文，但却只发送narrow的报文。
● compatible：可以接收或发送开销值类型为narrow和wide的报文。
● wide-compatible ：可以接收开销值类型为 narrow 和 wide 的报文，但却只发送wide的报文。
● wide：接收或发送开销值类型为wide的报文。
如果一端配置是narrow，另一端配置为wide或者wide-compatible，则两端不能互通。
如果一端配置是narrow-compatible，另一端配置为wide，则两端不能互通。
如果链路两端的设备的IS-IS开销类型不匹配，请执行cost-style命令修改配置。
步骤3 检查IS-IS路由是否为最优路由执行命令display isis route，查看IS-IS路由表。如果IS-IS路由表中存在指定的路由，执行display ip routing-table ip-address [ mask | mask-length ] verbose命令查看IP路由表中是否存在协议优先级比IS-IS高的路由。
说明State字段为Active Adv表示该路由为活跃的路由，如果存在相同前缀的多个协议的路由，协议优先级高的路由优选为活跃的路由。
如果在IP路由表中存在协议优先级比IS-IS高的路由，请根据网络的实际规划调整配置。
----结束
